Let's define `soft_score(a, bmax, lam)` = 2n * (bmax + log(mean exp(λ(b-bmax)))/λ) = 2n * soft_max. For large λ approx max. For acceptance and progress, track soft_score. But best exact uses max.

Function `_soft_max_and_grad(a, lam)`:
   fa = rfft(a, m)
   b = irfft(fa*fa, m)[:L]
   b = np.maximum(b,0)
   bmax = b.max()
   th = lam*(b-bmax)
   exp_th = np.exp(np.maximum(th, -50))
   z = exp_th.sum()
   p = exp_th/z
   ft = rfft(p, m)
   grad = 2*irfft(conj(fa)*ft, m)[:n]
   grad = np.maximum(grad,0) # maybe clip tiny negative
   soft = bmax + (np.log(z)/L) / lam  # since mean exp
   return b, bmax, p, fa, grad, soft

Pseudo for `_adam_local(a0, budget, seed, lam_start=8, lam_end=60, lr=0.15)`:
```
def _adam_local(a0, budget, rng, base_deadline):
    a = normalize(a0)
    n=len(a); L=2n-1; m=pow2
    y = np.log(a + 1e-300) - np.log(np.sum(np.exp(np.log(a+...)))) # stable:
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    y = loga - np.logaddexp.reduce(loga) # logsumexp
    state={m1:0,v1:0,t:0}
    best_surr = score_fft(a); best_a=a.copy()
    # pre allocate? 
    for step in range(max_iters):
       if now>deadline: break
       # softmax y
       ym = y - y.max()
       a = np.exp(ym); a /= a.sum()
       # compute b
       fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
       b = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, m)[:L]
       b = np.maximum(b, 0)
       bmax = b.max()
       # if bmax==0? 
       lam_factor = lam_start + (lam_end-lam_start)*min(1.0, step/total_steps)
       lam = lam_factor / max(bmax, 1e-15)
       th = lam*(b - bmax)
       expth = np.exp(th) # th≤0, maybe underflow okay
       s = expth.sum()
       if not finite or s==0: p = np.zeros; p[argmax(b)]=1
       else p=expth/s
       ft = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
       grad = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*ft, m)[:n]
       grad = np.where(grad>0, grad, 0.0)
       baseline = float(np.dot(a, grad))
       ygrad = a * (grad - baseline)
       # Adam update
       t=...
       y -= lr * ...
       # every 25 steps check if new score improved
       new_a = softmax(y)
       # We can recompute bmax? We already have old bmax not new. To check, compute new score via FFT (extra). Expensive but okay every step? We need for progress. But we can just accept and periodically compute score by recomputing? We need b for next iteration anyway. The Newton a used for next b will compute bmax. So we can track after update at top of loop.
       # To update best, compute bnew every N steps or if soft_score lower? We can compute bnew at end if time; cost one FFT. Maybe set `if step % 20 ==0 or improved_soft`.
    return best_a, best_surr
```

LP outer loop:
```
def _lp_improve(f_init, time_limit, rng):
    f = normalize_sum(f_init, S)
    M = max fft(f,f)
```

Let's write functions:

`_normalize_sum(a, S)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a,float).copy()
a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
s = a.sum()
if s <=1e-12:
    a.fill(1.0)
    s=a.sum()
a *= S/s
a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
return a
```

`_score_fft(a, m=None)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
n=a.size
if n==0: return inf
s=a.sum()
if s<0.01: return inf
if m is None: m=pow2(2*n-1)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)
b=b[:2*n-1]
M=b.max()
if M<0: M=0
return float(2*n*M/(s*s))
```
For normalized S, equals M but use general.

`_softmax_y(y)`:
```
y=np.maximum(y,-50); y-=y.max(); a=np.exp(y); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return ones/n; return a/s
```
Logsumexp stable for initial y:
```
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
y=loga - np.log(np.exp(loga).sum()) # but exp sum okay after subtract max:
lm=loga.max(); y=loga-lm - math.log(np.exp(loga-lm).sum())
```

Pseudo:
```
def _adam_optimize(a0, budget, rng, start_lam=8, end_lam=80, lr=0.12, b1=0.9, b2=0.999):
    start_t=time; best_a=a0.copy(); best_M=score_fft(a0)
    n=len(a0); S=math.sqrt(2*n); m=pow2(2*n-1)
    a=_normalize_sum(a0,S)
    y=_init_y(a)
    m1=np.zeros(n); v1=np.zeros(n); t=0
    st=start_t; last_best=st; stagn=0
    while time.monotonic() < start_t+budget:
       # occasionally add noise if stagnation?
       step = int((time.monotonic()-st)/0.002)
       frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-st)/max(1e-6,budget))
       lam_factor = start_lam + (end_lam-start_lam)*frac
       # compute current a score and fa
       a = _softmax_y(y) # maybe we keep a from previous; after update, we can set y and next loop; at start a current.
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
       b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m); b=b[:2*n-1]; b=np.maximum(b,0)
       M=float(b.max())
       if M < best_M: best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=time
       # if M not finite, break
       th = (lam_factor/max(M,1e-15))*(b-M)
       # avoid overflow: th<=0; exp okay
       expth=np.exp(th)
       z=expth.sum()
       if not finite or z<=0: p=np.zeros(2*n-1); idx=np.argmax(b); p[idx]=1
       else: p=expth/z
       ft=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
       grad=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*ft,m); grad=grad[:n]; grad=np.maximum(grad,0.0)
       baseline=float(np.dot(a,grad))
       ygrad=a*(grad-baseline)
       # Adam
       t+=1; m1=b1*m1+(1-b1)*ygrad; v1=b2*v1+(1-b2)*ygrad*ygrad
       mc=m1/(1-b1**t); vc=v1/(1-b2**t)
       y -= lr*mc/(np.sqrt(vc)+1e-8)
       # optionally clip y: y=clip(y,-50,50)
       # If stagnation, reset Adam? If now-last_best > 30s, perturb y and reset m,v? Maybe.
       if time.monotonic()-last_best > 25.0:
           # add gentle random perturb scaled by n? maybe 0.1
           y = y + 0.1*rng.normal(size=n)
           m1.fill(0); v1.fill(0); t=0
           last_best=time.monotonic()
```

Let's detail LP function:

```
def _lp_improve(f_init, budget, rng, consider):
    if budget<30 or 'sco' not in globals(): return
    n=len(f_init); S=math.sqrt(2*n); L=2*n-1
    f=_normalize_sum(f_init,S)
    M=_M_of_normalized(f) # via fft
    best_local=f.copy(); bestM=M
    # initial rows
    protect_step = max(1, L//800)
    protected = list(range(0,L, max(1,L//600))) maybe ~600
    top_active = np.argpartition(-b0, 200)[:200]
    rows = list(set(protected) | set(top_active.tolist()))
    if len(rows)>600: sample protected
    max_rows=800
    U=1000.0
    directions=[]
    t_start=time; lp_iter=0
    while time < deadline - maybe 120:
       if len(rows)>max_rows: # use last conv? we may not have last conv yet. postpone until after check.
       build A dense
       solve = linprog...
       if not optimal: break
       g=solve.x
       sumg=g.sum()
       if sumg<1e-8: break
       g_dir = g*S/sumg
       # add to directions every iteration? Cap 20.
       directions.append(g_dir)
       conv = _fft_conv(f,g) # full
       viol = conv - M
       maxv = viol.max()
       if maxv <= 1e-7*max(1.0,M): break
       # add rows
       addk = min(60, max_rows-len(rows))
       if addk>0:
           top_viol = np.argpartition(-viol, addk)[:addk]
           rows = list(set(rows) | top_viol)
       else:
           # remove slack and add top viol
           slack = M - conv
           # keep protected; candidate sort
           remove = len(rows) - max_rows + addk
           # if remove>0, sort nonprotected by slack desc remove
       lp_iter++
       if lp_iter > 200: break
       if time since solve > budget*0.5 maybe break
    # After solving (or break), collect unique directions (up to 12)
    for d in directions[-8:]:
       d = _normalize_sum(d,S)
       dirs = [d, (d+d[::-1])/2]
       for dir in dirs:
          for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,1.0):
             mix = (1-t)*f + t*dir
             mix = _normalize_sum(mix,S)
             Mmix = _M_of_normalized(mix)
             if Mmix < bestM or Mmix < best_exact_score? consider will handle.
             consider(mix, exact=False)
             if Mmix < best_local: best_local=mix.copy(); bestM=Mmix
    consider(best_local, exact=True)
```

Detailed `_scale_to`:
```
def _scale_to(a, target=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
        a.fill(np.maximum(0.01/n, 1e-12))*? # but sum >0.01
        a = np.ones(n)*max(1e-4, 0.01/n)
        return a
    if target is None: target=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    factor=target/s
    mx=a.max()
    if mx>0 and factor*mx > 1000.0:
        factor = min(factor, 1000.0/mx)
    a *= factor
    # ensure sum at least 0.01; if scaling made sum tiny? if a.sum() <0.01:
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2 < 0.01:
        # if possible, multiply to 0.01 not exceeding cap
        factor2=0.01/s2
        if factor2*a.max() <=1000.0:
            a *= factor2
        else:
            a = np.ones(n)*max(1e-4, 0.01/n)
    return np.clip(a,0,1000)
```
If target sum S <0.01 (n<3?), S=sqrt(2n) >=√2=1.414 for n≥1, so fine.

Softmax for random:

```
def _softmax_scale(y, S):
     y=np.clip(y,-50,50); y-=y.max(); a=np.exp(y); a/=a.sum(); return _scale_to(a,S)
```

`_score_fft` accept None:
```
def _score_fft(a):
   if a is None: return inf
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   n=a.size; m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)
   b=b[:2*n-1]
   M=b.max()
   if M<0: M=0
   if not np.isfinite(M): return inf
   return 2.0*n*M/(s*s)
```
For very small n, m=1? If n=1, 2n-1=1, pow2(1)=1. rfft length1 returns [a0]; irfft length1 -> [a0^2]; M=a0^2; score=2*1*a0^2/a0^2=2.

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(n):
    n=int(n)
    p=1
    while p<n: p<<=1
    return p
```
Can use bit_length:
```
return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()
```
For n=1, (0).bit_length()=0 ->1.

Final local perturb:
```
def _local_perturb(base, deadline):
   while time < deadline:
      a=base.copy()
      width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//400), max(2,n//20)))
      start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
      a[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.15))
      if rng.random()<0.3: a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
      a=_scale_to(a, math.sqrt(2*n)) # or keep sum
      consider(a, exact=False)
      if best_exact_arr is not None: base=best_exact_arr else base=best_sur_arr
```

```
import numpy as np
import time, math
try:
    import scipy.optimize as sco
except Exception:
    sco = None

def _next_pow2(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()

def _normalize_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if a.size==0: return None
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.full(a.size, max(1e-6, 0.01/a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1.0) # after /s max≤1, but if s clipped? still no >1000.
    return a
```

```
    s=a.sum()

       return np.full(y.size,1.0/y.size)
```

`consider` nested:
```
def consider(a, do_exact=False):
   nonlocal best_exact_arr, best_exact_score, best_sur_arr, best_sur_score, exact_calls
   if a is None: return
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if a.sum()<0.01: a=np.full(a.size, max(1e-6, 0.01/a.size))
   sc=_score_fft(a)
   if not np.isfinite(sc): return
   if sc < best_sur_score:
       best_sur_score=sc; best_sur_arr=a.copy()
       if do_exact or sc < best_exact_score - 1e-9:
           if exact_allowed():
               exact_calls +=1
               try: ex=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
               except Exception: ex=math.inf
               if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact_score:
                   best_exact_score=ex; best_exact_arr=a.copy()
```

```
if sc < best_sur_score: update sur
```

```
def _adam_optimize(a0, budget, rng, lam0=3.0, lam1=80.0, lr0=0.15, lr1=0.04):
    a0=_normalize_sum1(a0)
    if a0 is None: return None
    n=a0.size
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_score=_score_fft(a0)
    y=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
    m1=np.zeros(n); v1=np.zeros(n); t=0
    start=time.monotonic(); last_imp=start; noise=0
    L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now - start >= budget or now >= deadline - 30: break
       a=_softmax_y(y)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
       b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m); b=b[:L]
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
       M=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
       score=2.0*n*M
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_imp=now
       if M <=0: break
       frac=min(1.0,(now-start)/max(1e-9,budget))
       lam_factor=lam0+(lam1-lam0)*frac
       lam=lam_factor/max(M,1e-300)
       th=lam*(b-M)
       # to avoid huge, th min e.g., -745
       th=np.clip(th, -745.0, 0.0)
       et=np.exp(th)
       z=et.sum()
       if z<=0 or not np.isfinite(z):
           p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
       else:
           p=et/z
       ft=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
       grad=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*ft,m)
       grad=grad[:n]
       grad=np.maximum(grad,0.0)
       baseline=float(np.dot(a,grad))
       ygrad=a*(grad-baseline)
       t+=1
       m1=0.9*m1+0.1*ygrad
       v1=0.999*v1+0.001*ygrad*ygrad
       bc1=1.0-0.9**t
       bc2=1.0-0.999**t
       lr=lr0+(lr1-lr0)*frac
       y = y - lr * (m1/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v1/bc2)+1e-8)
       if (t & 63)==0:
           y = y - np.mean(y)
           y = np.clip(y, -80, 80)
       if now - last_imp > 45.0 and noise < 15:
           y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n)
           m1.fill(0.0); v1.fill(0.0); t=0
           last_imp=now; noise +=1
       if now - start > 180.0 and now - last_imp > 120.0:
           break
    return best_a
```
If `b` has small negative and M 0? no.
Potential performance: `0.9**t` each iter okay. Could maintain bias factors.
If budget very small, start frac 1; okay.
If no improvement for 120s after start >180, break; but if budget 300s and no improve at 300, returns best. Good.

```
def _lp_improve(f0, budget, rng):
   if sco is None or budget < 60: return
   f=np.asarray(f0,float).ravel()
   n=f.size
   if n > 12000 or n < 2: return
   S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   f=_scale_to(f,S)
   if f is None: return
   L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(L)
   # Compute M and rows
   fa=np.fft.rfft(f,m); b0=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)[:L]; b0=np.maximum(b0,0); M=float(b0.max())
   if not finite M or M<=0: return
   # rows
   protn = 220
   protected = list(np.linspace(0,L-1,protn,dtype=int))
   topk = min(120, L)
   active = list(np.argpartition(-b0, topk)[:topk].astype(int))
   rows = list(dict.fromkeys(protected + active))
   max_rows=450
   U=1000.0
   directions=[]
   start=time.monotonic(); it=0
   lastf = f.copy(); lastM=M; lastfa=fa
   while time.monotonic() < start+budget and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       it+=1
       if len(rows) > max_rows: # before building? Need conv to remove; use last conv? Not available initial; skip removal until after check. But rows initial 340<max.
       # Build A
       A = np.empty((len(rows), n), dtype=np.float64)
       for r,k in enumerate(rows):
           row=A[r]; row.fill(0.0)
           st=max(0,k-n+1); en=min(n-1,k)
           if st<=en:
              high=k-st; low=k-en
              if high==low: row[st]=f[high]
              else:
                 stop = None if low==0 else low-1
                 row[st:en+1] = f[high:stop:-1]
       b_ub = (M + 1e-12*max(1.0,M))*np.ones(len(rows))
       c = np.ones(n)
       try:
           res = sco.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,U), method='highs')
       except Exception:
           break
       if res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
           # If unbounded? break
           break
       g = np.asarray(res.x,float).copy()
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
       g = np.clip(g,0.0,1000.0) # before rescale; sum can be >? If cap here maybe alter LP; do not cap? Since variables could be >1000? U=1000 so okay.
       sg = float(np.sum(g))
       if sg <= 1e-12: break
       g_dir = g * (float(np.sum(f))) / sg
       directions.append(g_dir)
       if len(directions)>8: directions.pop(0)
       # Check full feasibility
       fg = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.fft.rfft(g,m), m)[:L]
       fg = np.maximum(fg,0.0)
       viol = fg - M
       maxv = float(np.max(viol)) if viol.size else 0.0
       if maxv <= 1e-7*max(1.0,M):
           break
       addk = min(30, max(1, max_rows - len(rows) + 10)) # allow exceed?
       top = np.argpartition(-viol, min(addk, L-1))[:addk]
       new_rows = list(set(rows) | set(int(x) for x in top))
       if len(new_rows) > max_rows:
           # keep protected and top active
           prot_set=set(protected)
           slack = M - fg
           # sort candidates by slack descending (remove most slack)
           candidates = [(float(slack[i]), i) for i in new_rows if i not in prot_set]
           candidates.sort(reverse=True)
           remove = len(new_rows) - max_rows
           rem=set([i for _,i in candidates[:remove]])
           new_rows = [i for i in new_rows if i not in rem]
       rows = new_rows
       if it > 40: break
   # line search directions
   for d in directions:
       d=_scale_to(d, math.sqrt(2.0*n))
       if d is None: continue
       dirs=[d, 0.5*(d+d[::-1])]
       # smooth
       if n>=10:
          kernel=np.ones(min(9,n if n%2 else n-1) or 1)/5.0? Need length odd.
```

```
          if klen>1:
              ds=np.convolve(d, np.ones(klen)/klen, mode='same')
              dirs.append(np.maximum(ds,0.0))
       for dir in dirs:
          dir=_scale_to(dir, math.sqrt(2.0*n))
          if dir is None: continue
          for tt in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0):
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sco
except Exception:
    sco = None


def _next_pow2(n: int) -> int:
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _normalize_sum1(a):
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, max(1e-12, 0.01 / a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)


def _scale_to(a, target=None):
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a.fill(0.0)
        s = 0.0
    if target is None:
        target = math.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, a.size))
    if s < 0.01:
        val = max(1e-12, 0.01 / a.size)
        if val * a.size > 1000.0 * a.size:
            val = 1000.0 / a.size
        return np.full(a.size, max(1e-15, val), dtype=np.float64)
    factor = float(target) / s
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 1.0
    if mx > 0.0 and factor * mx > 1000.0:
        factor = min(factor, 1000.0 / mx)
    a = a * factor
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 < 0.01:
        f2 = 0.01 / s2
        mx2 = float(a.max()) if a.size else 1.0
        if mx2 > 0.0 and f2 * mx2 > 1000.0:
            f2 = min(f2, 1000.0 / mx2)
        a = a * max(f2, 1.0)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 < 0.01:
            a = np.full(a.size, max(1e-15, 0.01 / a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _softmax_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -80.0)
    mx = float(np.max(y))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.clip(y - mx, -745.0, 700.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _score_fft(a):
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, m)
    b = b[:L]
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return math.inf
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))


def _log_b2_exp(a, m=None):
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return None, None, None
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if m is None:
        m = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, m)[:L]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b, fa, m


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(max(1.0, budget_s))
    hard_end = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    global_best_exact_arr = None
    global_best_exact_score = math.inf
    global_best_sur_arr = None
    global_best_sur_score = math.inf
    exact_calls = 0
    MAX_EXACT = 120

    def exact_allowed():
        return (
            exact_calls < MAX_EXACT
            and time.monotonic() < hard_end - 8.0
        )

    def consider(a, do_exact=False):
        nonlocal (
            global_best_exact_arr,
            global_best_exact_score,
            global_best_sur_arr,
            global_best_sur_score,
            exact_calls,
        )
        a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
        if a is None or a.size == 0:
            return
        n = a.size
        if a.sum() < 0.01:
            a = np.full(n, max(1e-15, 0.01 / n), dtype=np.float64)
        sc = _score_fft(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return

        if do_exact or sc < global_best_exact_score - 1e-10:
            if exact_allowed():
                exact_calls += 1
                try:
                    ex = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
                except Exception:
                    ex = math.inf
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < global_best_exact_score:
                    global_best_exact_score = ex
                    global_best_exact_arr = a.copy()

        if sc < global_best_sur_score:
            global_best_sur_score = sc
            global_best_sur_arr = a.copy()

    def current_best_for_local():
        if global_best_exact_arr is not None:
            return global_best_exact_arr
        if global_best_sur_arr is not None:
            return global_best_sur_arr
        return None

    # Parent / fallback base
    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(parent).all():
        parent = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n0 = int(parent.size)
    S0 = math.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, n0))
    base = _scale_to(parent, S0)
    if base is None:
        base = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) * math.sqrt(2.0 * 256 / 256.0)
        n0 = 256

    # Quick diverse candidates
    quick = []
    if base is not None:
        quick.append(base.copy())
        quick.append(base[::-1].copy())
        quick.append(0.5 * (base + base[::-1]))
        bsorted = np.sort(base)
        quick.append(bsorted.copy())
        quick.append(bsorted[::-1].copy())
        for p in (0.5, 2.0, 3.0):
            acc = np.power(base + 1e-12, p)
            acc = _scale_to(acc, S0)
            if acc is not None:
                quick.append(acc)

        i = np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64)
        for beta in (0.6, 1.5, 3.5, 7.0):
            quick.append(_scale_to(np.exp(-beta * i / max(1, n0 - 1)), S0))
            quick.append(_scale_to(np.exp(-beta * (1.0 - i / max(1, n0 - 1))), S0))
        for c in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
            for sigma in (0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
                quick.append(_scale_to(np.exp(-0.5 * ((i / max(1.0, n0 - 1.0) - c) / sigma) ** 2), S0))
        for k in (1, 2, 3):
            for alpha in (0.35, 0.70, 0.95):
                a = 1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * i / max(1, n0 - 1.0))
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                quick.append(_scale_to(a, S0))
                a = 1.0 + alpha * np.abs(np.sin(math.pi * k * i / max(1, n0)))
                quick.append(_scale_to(a, S0))

        for _ in range(8):
            y = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n0)
            y = 0.4 * y + 0.6 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * i / max(1, n0 - 1.0))
            quick.append(_scale_to(_softmax_y(y), S0))
        for _ in range(4):
            y = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n0)
            y += 0.5 * p * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i / max(1, n0 - 1.0)) * 0
            quick.append(_scale_to(_softmax_y(0.8 * y), S0))

    # Small-length probes (cheap, and sometimes useful if short sequences are better)
    for ns in (8, 16, 29, 58, 97, 199):
        Ssmall = math.sqrt(2.0 * ns)
        ism = np.arange(ns, dtype=np.float64)
        quick.append(_scale_to(np.ones(ns), Ssmall))
        quick.append(_scale_to(np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, ns), Ssmall))
        for k in (1, 2):
            for alpha in (0.4, 0.8):
                quick.append(_scale_to(np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * ism / max(1, ns)), 0.0), Ssmall))
        for _ in range(12):
            y = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=ns)
            y += 0.3 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * ism / max(1.0, ns))
            quick.append(_scale_to(_softmax_y(y), Ssmall))

    quick_scores = []
    for a in quick:
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = _score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            quick_scores.append((sc, a))
    quick_scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for sc, a in quick_scores[:20]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
            break
        consider(a, do_exact=True)

    if current_best_for_local() is None:
        consider(base, do_exact=True)
        if current_best_for_local() is None:
            consider(np.ones(128) * (math.sqrt(2.0 * 128) / 128.0), do_exact=True)

    # Adam / mirror-descent local optimizer on log-softmax variables
    def adam_optimize(a0, budget, rng_local):
        if budget <= 2.0:
            return None
        a = _normalize_sum1(a0)
        if a is None:
            return None
        n = a.size
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = 2.0 * n * (max(0.0, float(np.max(a * a))) if n == 1 else 0.0)
        # compute real initial score
        init_sc = _score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(init_sc):
            best_score = init_sc

        y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        y = y - float(np.mean(y))
        m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        t = 0
        a0t = time.monotonic()
        last_imp = a0t
        noise_count = 0
        L = 2 * n - 1
        m = _next_pow2(L)

        while time.monotonic() < a0t + budget and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            a = _softmax_y(y)
            b, fa, _ = _log_b2_exp(a, m)
            if b is None:
                break
            M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                break
            score = 2.0 * n * M
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_imp = time.monotonic()

            frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - a0t) / max(1e-9, float(budget)))
            lam_factor = 3.0 + (80.0 - 3.0) * frac
            lam = lam_factor / max(M, 1e-300)
            th = lam * (b - M)
            th = np.clip(th, -745.0, 0.0)
            et = np.exp(th)
            z = float(et.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
                p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
            else:
                p = et / z

            ft = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
            grad = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conjugate(fa) * ft, m)[:n]
            grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
            grad = np.maximum(grad, 0.0)
            baseline = float(np.dot(a, grad))
            ygrad = a * (grad - baseline)

            t += 1
            m1 = 0.90 * m1 + 0.10 * ygrad
            v1 = 0.999 * v1 + 0.001 * (ygrad * ygrad)
            bc1 = 1.0 - 0.90 ** t
            bc2 = 1.0 - 0.999 ** t
            lr = 0.15 + (0.04 - 0.15) * frac
            d = (m1 / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v1 / bc2) + 1e-8)
            y = y - lr * d

            if (t & 63) == 0:
                y = y - float(np.mean(y))
                y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 80.0)

            if time.monotonic() - last_imp > 45.0 and noise_count < 16:
                y = y + rng_local.normal(0.0, 0.22, size=n)
                m1.fill(0.0)
                v1.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                last_imp = time.monotonic()
                noise_count += 1

            if (time.monotonic() - a0t) > 150.0 and (time.monotonic() - last_imp) > 110.0:
                break

        return best_a

    # Run Adam on a few starts
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0:
        ins = current_best_for_local()
        starts = []
        if ins is not None:
            starts.append(ins.copy())

        if n0 > 4:
            x = np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64)
            starts.append(_softmax_y(0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x / max(1, n0 - 1)) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n0)))
            starts.append(_softmax_y(0.35 * np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * x / max(1, n0 - 1)) - 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x / max(1, n0 - 1)) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n0)))
            starts.append(_softmax_y(0.75 * (1.0 - x / max(1.0, n0))))
            starts.append(_scale_to(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.65 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x / max(1, n0 - 1)), 0.0), math.sqrt(2.0 * n0)))

        seen = []
       预算 = 0
        adam_total = min(450.0, max(0.0, float(deadline - start) * 0.5 - 40.0))
        if adam_total > 60.0 and starts:
            per = adam_total / float(len(starts))
            for astart in starts:
                key = tuple(np.round(np.asarray(astart, dtype=np.float64)[: min(32, 32)], 6))
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.append(key)
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 80.0:
                    break
                res = adam_optimize(astart, max(5.0, per), rng)
                if res is not None:
                    consider(res, do_exact=True)

    # LP pivot / cutting-plane-inspired improvement
    def lp_improve(f0, budget, rng_lp):
        if sco is None or budget < 45.0:
            return
        f = _scale_to(f0, math.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, f0.size)))
        if f is None:
            return
        n = f.size
        if n < 4 or n > 12000:
            return
        L = 2 * n - 1
        m = _next_pow2(L)
        b0, fa, _ = _log_b2_exp(f, m)
        if b0 is None:
            return
        M = float(np.max(b0))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            return

        prot = list(np.linspace(0, L - 1, min(180, L), dtype=int))
        topk = min(80, L)
        if topk < L:
            active = list(np.argpartition(-b0, topk - 1)[:topk].astype(int))
        else:
            active = list(np.argsort(-b0)[:topk].astype(int))
        rows = list(dict.fromkeys(prot + active))
        max_rows = 350
        S = float(f.sum())
        U = min(1000.0, max(50.0, 2.0 * S))
        directions = []
        a0t = time.monotonic()
        it = 0
        base_f = f.copy()
        base_M = M
        base_fa = fa

        while time.monotonic() < a0t + budget and time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
            it += 1
            if len(rows) > max_rows:
                # Use a dummy uniform slack estimate only if no convolution is available yet.
                if not hasattr(lp_improve, "_last_fg") or lp_improve._last_fg is None:
                    cand = [r for r in rows if r not in set(prot)]
                    cand.sort()
                    rows = prot + cand[: max(0, max_rows - len(prot))]
                    continue

            try:
                A = np.empty((len(rows), n), dtype=np.float64)
                for r, kk in enumerate(rows):
                    row = A[r]
                    row.fill(0.0)
                    st = max(0, kk - n + 1)
                    en = min(n - 1, kk)
                    if st <= en:
                        high = kk - st
                        low = kk - en
                        if high == low:
                            row[st] = f[high]
                        else:
                            stop = None if low == 0 else low - 1
                            row[st:en + 1] = f[high:stop:-1]
                b_ub = (M + 1e-11 + 1e-9 * max(1.0, M)) * np.ones(len(rows))
                res = sco.linprog(
                    np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=(0.0, U),
                    method="highs",
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            if not hasattr(res, "x") or getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0:
                break
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
            sg = float(g.sum())
            if sg <= 1e-12:
                break
            g_dir = g * (S / sg)
            directions.append(g_dir)
            if len(directions) > 8:
                directions.pop(0)

            fg = np.fft.irfft(base_fa * np.fft.rfft(g, m), m)[:L]
            fg = np.where(np.isfinite(fg), fg, 0.0)
            fg = np.maximum(fg, 0.0)
            lp_improve._last_fg = fg
            viol = fg - M
            maxv = float(np.max(viol)) if viol.size else 0.0
            if maxv <= 1e-7 * max(1.0, M):
                break

            take = min(20, max(1, max_rows - len(rows) + 8), L)
            if take >= L:
                top = np.argsort(-viol)
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(-viol, take - 1)[:take]
            new_rows = list(dict.fromkeys(rows + [int(x) for x in top]))
            if len(new_rows) > max_rows:
                prot_set = set(prot)
                slack = M - fg
                try:
                    cand = sorted(
                        (float(slack[i]), i) for i in new_rows if i not in prot_set
                    )
                    # remove most slack first
                    cand.sort(key=lambda z: -z[0])
                except Exception:
                    cand = []
                rem = len(new_rows) - max_rows
                to_remove = set([i for _, i in cand[:rem]])
                new_rows = [i for i in new_rows if i not in to_remove]
            rows = new_rows

            if it > 30:
                break

        for d in directions[-6:]:
            d = _scale_to(d, math.sqrt(2.0 * n))
            if d is None:
                continue
            dlist = [d]
            sym = 0.5 * (d + d[::-1])
            dlist.append(sym)
            if n >= 5:
                ds = np.convolve(d, np.ones(5) / 5.0, mode="same")
                dlist.append(np.maximum(ds, 0.0))

            fref = base_f.copy()
            for dirr in dlist:
                dirr = _scale_to(dirr, math.sqrt(2.0 * n))
                if dirr is None:
                    continue
                for tt in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 1.0):
                    mix = (1.0 - tt) * fref + tt * dirr
                    consider(mix, do_exact=False)
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 55.0:
                        break
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 55.0:
                    break

        consider(base_f, do_exact=True)

    # Apply LP to current best, maybe repeat once if time remains
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        best_in = current_best_for_local()
        if best_in is not None:
            lp_budget = min(350.0, max(60.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55 - 45.0))
            lp_improve(best_in, lp_budget, rng)
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
                best_in = current_best_for_local()
                if best_in is not None:
                    lp_budget2 = min(220.0, max(45.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.5 - 45.0))
                    lp_improve(best_in, lp_budget2, rng)

    # Final cheap local polish
    base_final = current_best_for_local()
    if base_final is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        last_final = time.monotonic()
        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
            a = base_final.copy()
            n = a.size
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 400), max(1, n // 24)))
            width = max(1, min(n, width))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            en = min(n, st + width)
            a[st:en] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.16)))
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
            a = _scale_to(a, math.sqrt(2.0 * n))
            consider(a, do_exact=False)
            if global_best_exact_arr is not None and np.array_equal(global_best_exact_arr, a):
                pass
            base_final = current_best_for_local()
            if time.monotonic() - last_final > 60.0:
                # avoid degrading pathological infinite loop
                if global_best_sur_score > 1.999999:
                    base_final = base_final.copy()
                last_final = time.monotonic()

    # Final validation and return
    if global_best_exact_arr is None and global_best_sur_arr is not None:
        consider(global_best_sur_arr, do_exact=True)

    if global_best_exact_arr is None:
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) * (math.sqrt(2.0 * 256) / 256.0)
        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            s = math.inf
        global_best_exact_arr = fallback
        global_best_exact_score = s if np.isfinite(s) else math.inf

    out = np.asarray(global_best_exact_arr, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if out.size == 0 or float(out.sum()) < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) * (math.sqrt(2.0 * 128) / 128.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
